Three trees in ONE process — pre-fix (`main`'s `src/`, `moves.ts` at `fb4fa40934e768dffeaeed0b43548842`), delivered, and a **BYTE-IDENTICAL TWIN of the delivered tree as a NULL CONTROL BY IDENTITY** — 7 rounds, tree AND cell order rotated every round, discarded warm-up, medians of per-round rates, node counts checksummed across every tree and round (238,936,362 nodes). **THE CATALOG'S STANDING CAUTION WAS FOLLOWED AND IT IS WHY NO PER-CELL CLAIM IS MADE: the structurally-zero cells were read FIRST** — `position-3` and `position-6` carry no castling rights and `makeMove` can only clear rights, never create them, so the two added byte compares execute ZERO times in those subtrees; their true ratio is exactly 1.0000 and they measured 1.0084 / 0.9918 / 1.0041 / 0.9989, while the identity twin read 1.0087 and 0.9983 aggregate and up to 1.0126 per cell. **The instrument's own floor is therefore about ±1% per cell, larger than any signal** — the bar is met with margin and no per-cell regression or improvement is claimed, the kiwipete 0.9930 reading in particular sitting inside noise established by cells whose delta is provably zero. **Denominator fence carried: 97.3% of the nodes perft counts are free leaves, so this is generation-with-free-leaves and is NEVER evidence for `NFR-005`.** `NFR-005` was NOT measured and the key benchmark was NOT re-run — the exclusion is structural, exactly as the ticket instructs. **Deep perft 32/32 EXACT (36.93 s), run BEFORE review; nobody had run it on either tree for this ticket.** No wall-clock assertion was added to the suite, and `plans/nfr-catalog.md` was NOT edited. **`NFR-001` WAS NOT RE-MEASURED BY QE, DELIBERATELY — Security/NFR follows and owns it, and NO throughput figure in any QE output is QE's own (`npm run perft` was not run by that role).** The part of the Engineer's measurement QE flags as load-bearing is its CAVEAT: the instrument's per-cell floor is ~±1%, established by two structurally-zero cells and an identity twin reading up to 1.0126 — larger than any signal the change could produce — so **kiwipete's 0.9930 is NOT a 0.7% regression** and no per-cell number is claimable;
